# Complete score chain review

Production project `cadernin-6c0d1`; metadata read at 2026-11-01T01:15:36.707Z. No data was changed.

## Reading this report

- **Existing history** means revision documents under one score ID. All included score containers currently have one revision: no multi-revision chains were found.
- **Title-match candidate** means separate score IDs have the same title after ignoring case, accents, whitespace and punctuation. This identifies possible relationships, not musical identity or a proven revision sequence.
- Original titles, score IDs and files are preserved. Different title spellings remain visible in every group.
- Reviewed predecessor links prefer the nearest available earlier year within the same destination project. Main annual songbooks take precedence over special songbooks when the prior year has multiple matches. No title renames or merges are performed; file contents were not compared.
- Madeira Pirata and the four rejected Garota duplicates listed in inputs/review-decisions.json are excluded from this report. The canonical 2024 and hyphenated 2025/2026 copies are retained. Reviewed title-variation equivalences are approved for linking only; every original spelling remains visible.
- Previously reviewed cross-project references from garota/2025, na-tora/2025 and queimando/2024 to carnaval-bh/2024 are used when no same-project earlier-year match exists. Original titles are preserved.
- Reviewed tags: zagateile/acervo → axé; besourinhos/2024 → beatles; carnaval-bh/festa-junina-2024 → 6 baião, 7 xote, 4 arrasta-pé. Original production tags remain visible separately.
